# Seed 60 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - the anchoring test, clean: small at both widths, so no evidence of anchoring; its fastest +3.6%, under the bar (Iteration 406)

`seed 60 tag2 12h widths noevolved then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --widths 8,4 --frontier --cpus 2,8` at
bcb34da3e (Iteration 398): no design from an earlier run's record - the hand-made designs and the founders built from
them - with the tag-2 requirement, at both widths; the first run of the after-seed-59 batch. The remote checks started
03:53:24, the evolution 03:58:49; it evolved 8 h 50 min (seed 59: 8 h 55). 34,598 designs, generations 0-138: 26,171
alive, 8,180 screened (24.5%), 247 refused at the reach limit.

**The verdict - small at both widths.** At 64 bits from both CPUs (the owner's rule of 389): fastest +3.6%, smallest
-11.20%, memory -4 KB, hypervolume +0.4%, best Q -5.3% - small, and small on each CPU alone (cpu 2 +2.4%, cpu 8 +3.3%).
At 32 bits its fastest 0.325 (4ac602c8bd), 3.5% behind the others' best. The Q-best of all runs stays seed 57's
e6b5f1749e (Q 0.209 of s6's). By the review's 3c no gain past a bar, so no evidence of anchoring at 12 hours: from the
hand-made designs alone it reached the carried runs' fastest - and passed it, under the bar - and stayed 11% above
the small end, seed 58's plain designs (the requirement's cost, as seed 59).

**Facts.** Its front: 167 designs, all tag-2; tos on 110, the JIT on 58, tail and msc on none; bss and thinhdr on
every design, lean on 165. heldout (sieve): Spearman 0.958, the pick's regret +1.5%. Diversity 0.48 at the end; the
smallest still gaining (its last gain at generation 136 of 138), the best speed's last 2% at 104. At 32 bits 1,042 died
on the corpus - all bfind without tos - and 484 did not compile - all tail: GCC 161, clang 255, clang with preserve_none
68 - the two classes the after-seed-62 batch fixes; 150 of the front have a 32-bit speed.

---

# Evolved VM designs

26418 designs evaluated, 26171 alive, 8180 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 79827608b1, speed 0.124, binary + image 30,364, memory 168 KB - Q 0.189 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
